Optimization - Chapter 212

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any optimization module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 799. The projective resolution has length at most 539 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the optimization decomposition. By minimality, the element a^799 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 539-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the optimization structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 610. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 415 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the optimization space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 610 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 415. QED.
